## Inference Research Guidance Check

- Original private/member spelling is unavailable. `m_text` is selected because current derived formal source already accesses that exact protected member, while constructor/destructor/accessor/paint all use one owned pointer at `+0x10c`.
- The base is `ButtonControlPane`, not flat `ControlPane`, because the constructor calls the exact base helper, temporarily installs all three ButtonControl vptr views, and every direct complete allocation is `0x110` versus the proven `0x10c` base size.
- Constructor bounds remain `const RectBounds *`, not an invented reference API, because direct callers and the derived class formal use pointer form. The body dereferences it only to call the current base reference-form constructor.
- `GetText` deliberately preserves the observed character-count/byte-count mismatch. Correcting it to `copied * sizeof(wchar_t)` would be a behavior change unsupported by the binary.
- `FillRect`, rectangle helpers, inherited draw-state fields, and `DrawTextInRect` use established current project source-facing names. Raw callback/global or `sub_` spellings are not promoted.
- Colors, visual-state 11, label half-height 6, and one-pixel offsets remain local constants. No source global/static/table is invented for immediates.
- Vtable, RTTI, adjusted deleting entries, scalar deleting wrapper, and CC fences are compiler/linker products. Their human cause is the virtual class declaration and ordinary destructor.

## Positive Evidence Summary
Unless otherwise noted, the evidence in this section is a dated 2026-08-14 research/callback snapshot rather than a permanent-current repository assertion.
- Four contiguous authored functions occupy one closed generic-control band and are followed immediately by TextButtonEx constructor at `0x00495450`.
- Constructor, ordinary destructor, and scalar wrapper independently write callable heads `0x00617c7c`, `0x00617ce8`, and `0x00617d18`, proving complete-object and inherited EventHandler/TimerHandler views.
- Every observed direct complete-object allocation for the generic class is `0x110`; the dated base-layout snapshot is `0x10c`; the sole derived field is the pointer at `+0x10c`.
- Constructor allocation/copy, destructor free, GetText read/copy, and OnPaint read all converge on the same owned wide string.
- Exact primary slots identify scalar deletion, OnPaint, and GetText; secondary/tertiary deleting cells identify both adjustors. Vtable-only method reachability is expected, not dead code.
- Generic RTTI names distinguish `TextButtonControlPane` from its FittingRoom derived class and from adjacent TextButtonEx.
- The dated 2026-08-14 derived formal-source snapshot independently confirms constructor pointer form and protected `m_text` access.
- The dated 2026-08-13 Gate 1 generated snapshot physically proved the reconstruction defect: compiler marker present, all four bodies absent, and required header absent. The dated 2026-08-14 generated snapshot physically proves closure: all four definitions and the expanded UID0002YE covered-by marker are present in CPP, and the complete class declaration/size invariant is present in H.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis
Ownership conclusions below use the dated 2026-08-14 evidence snapshot and remain technical recommendations rather than lifecycle claims.
### 1. Dedicated `ui/controls/TextButtonControlPane.cpp/.h`

- Evidence for: dedicated by-file route in the dated snapshot; generic RTTI; narrow contiguous four-method band; broad cross-feature constructor use; derived classes require a shared declaration; proposed source tree; direct class/emitter chain.
- Evidence against: physical adjacency to ButtonControl and TextButtonEx could reflect one original compilation unit; original PDB/source paths are unavailable.
- Decision: strongest supported source-facing placement. Retain UID0000OK and the required sibling H recorded by the dated applied result.

### 2. Broader `ButtonControlPane.cpp/.h`

- Evidence for: constructor calls ButtonControl setup; adjacent binary ordering; the dated `ButtonControlPane.h` snapshot contains TextButtonEx and related class declarations.
- Evidence against: project already has a dedicated TextButton by-file route; generic class has enough authored code for a narrow file; source tree separates it; folding would invalidate a gate-valid direct route without new evidence.
- Decision: reject for this assignment. Keep ButtonControl as dependency only.

### 3. FittingRoom or another feature module

- Evidence for: ordinary destructor refs and a derived class appear in FittingRoom; several construction consumers are dialogs/features.
- Evidence against: ten cross-feature constructors, generic RTTI/vtables, separate derived RTTI/source, and reusable control behavior. Consumer xrefs do not confer source ownership.
- Decision: reject feature ownership.

### Proposed new file/grouping, if applicable

- No new by-file UID is needed. The existing UID0000OK route is correct.
- Add only the physical sibling header output implied by UID0000EJ H formal source.
- Likely full contents: one complete class declaration; constructor; destructor; GetText; OnPaint; compiler-covered child marker.
- Rejected contents: SetTextResource, DrawTextInRect body, vtable/RTTI arrays, scalar/adjustor bodies, resource literals, FittingRoom derived methods, and unrelated ButtonControl/TextButtonEx source.

Exact UID0000EJ `RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE` insertion:

```cpp
#include "TextButtonControlPane.h"
#include <wchar.h>
#include "../../util/MemoryMan.h"

TextButtonControlPane::TextButtonControlPane(const wchar_t *text,
                                             const RectBounds *bounds)
    : ButtonControlPane(*bounds)
{
    MemoryMan *memoryMan = GetMemoryMan();
    const size_t byteCount = (wcslen(text) + 1) * sizeof(wchar_t);

    m_text = static_cast<wchar_t *>(
        GetMemoryMan()->AllocateBufferMemory(byteCount));
    memoryMan->MemmoveWrapper(m_text, text, byteCount);
}

TextButtonControlPane::~TextButtonControlPane()
{
    if (m_text != 0) {
        m_text = static_cast<wchar_t *>(
            GetMemoryMan()->FreeBufferMemory(m_text));
    }
}

short TextButtonControlPane::GetText(wchar_t *destination,
                                     short maxCharacters)
{
    short copied = maxCharacters;
    const short textLength = static_cast<short>(wcslen(m_text));
    if (copied > textLength) {
        copied = textLength;
    }

    GetMemoryMan()->MemmoveWrapper(destination, m_text, copied);
    destination[copied] = L'\0';
    return copied;
}

void TextButtonControlPane::OnPaint()
{
    const unsigned int kNormalTopLeftColor = 192;
    const unsigned int kNormalBottomRightColor = 200;
    const unsigned int kPressedTopLeftColor = 200;
    const unsigned int kPressedBottomRightColor = 192;
    const unsigned int kButtonFillColor = 196;
    const unsigned int kEnabledTextColor = 128;
    const unsigned int kDisabledTextColor = 134;
    const unsigned char kPressedVisualState = 11;
    const int kLabelHalfHeight = 6;

    const bool pressed = (m_visualState == kPressedVisualState);
    const unsigned int topLeftColor =
        pressed ? kPressedTopLeftColor : kNormalTopLeftColor;
    const unsigned int bottomRightColor =
        pressed ? kPressedBottomRightColor : kNormalBottomRightColor;
    const unsigned int textColor =
        m_enabled ? kEnabledTextColor : kDisabledTextColor;

    RectBounds frame = m_visibleBounds;

    SetDrawColor(kButtonFillColor);
    FillRect(&frame);

    MoveTo(frame.left, frame.bottom - 1);
    SetDrawColor(topLeftColor);
    LineTo(frame.left, frame.top);
    LineTo(frame.right - 1, frame.top);
    SetDrawColor(bottomRightColor);
    LineTo(frame.right - 1, frame.bottom - 1);
    LineTo(frame.left, frame.bottom - 1);

    InsetRect(&frame, 1, 1);

    MoveTo(frame.left, frame.bottom - 1);
    SetDrawColor(topLeftColor);
    LineTo(frame.left, frame.top);
    LineTo(frame.right - 1, frame.top);
    SetDrawColor(bottomRightColor);
    LineTo(frame.right - 1, frame.bottom - 1);
    LineTo(frame.left, frame.bottom - 1);

    RectBounds textRect = m_visibleBounds;
    const int textCenterY = (textRect.top + textRect.bottom) / 2;
    textRect.top = textCenterY - kLabelHalfHeight;
    textRect.bottom = textCenterY + kLabelHalfHeight;

    if (pressed) {
        OffsetRect(&textRect, 1, 1);
    }

    SetTextColor(textColor);
    m_textDrawMode = 1;
    m_textAlign = 1;
    DrawTextInRect(m_text, static_cast<int>(wcslen(m_text)), &textRect);

    if (m_enabled && m_controlSelected) {
        SetDrawColor(1);
        m_drawMode = 0;
        DrawRectFrame(&m_visibleBounds);
    }

    if (m_enabled && m_controlActive) {
        SetDrawColor(128);
        m_drawMode = 0;
        DrawRectFrame(&m_visibleBounds);
    }
}
```
